**Note**

-   This interface sets H.264protocolencoding channel's transform 、 amount/quantity convert's configure。
-   变换、量化属性主要由六个参数组成： 
    -   intra\_trans\_mode: frameintra-prediction macroblock 's transform attribute。intra\_trans\_mode = 0indicates supportsfor/to frameintra-prediction macroblock supports4x4transform and 8x8transform ；intra\_trans\_mode = 1indicates onlysupportsfor/to frameintra-prediction macroblock supports4x4transform ；intra\_trans\_mode = 2indicates onlysupportsfor/to frameintra-prediction macroblock supports8x8transform 。onlyhas/have in/at encoding channelprotocolis/for high profileand svc-t when, before select 8x8transform , i.e., in/at baseline profileand main profile under, system onlysupportsintra\_trans\_mode = 1's configure。
    -   inter\_trans\_mode: frameinter-prediction macroblock 's transform attribute。inter\_trans\_mode = 0indicates supportsfor/to frameinter-prediction macroblock supports4x4transform and 8x8transform ；inter\_trans\_mode = 1indicates onlysupportsfor/to frameinter-prediction macroblock supports4x4transform ；inter\_trans\_mode = 2indicates onlysupportsfor/to frameinter-prediction macroblock supports8x8transform 。onlyhas/have in/at encoding channelprotocolis/for high profileand svc-t when, before select 8x8transform , i.e., in/at baseline profileand main profile under, system onlysupportsinter\_trans\_mode = 1's configure。
    -   scaling\_list\_valid: indicates inter\_scaling\_list8x8、intra\_scaling\_list8x8quantization tablewhether has/have effect.not supportedSets scaling\_list\_validis/for true。
    -   inter\_scaling\_list8x8：对帧间预测宏块进行8x8变换时，可由用户通过此数组提供量化表，保留，暂时没有使用。
    -   intra\_scaling\_list8x8：对帧内预测宏块进行8x8变换时，可由用户通过此数组提供量化表，保留，暂时没有使用。
    -   chroma\_qp\_index\_offset: specific meaning refer to H.264protocol。

-   this interfaceis an advanced interface，users may selectively call，not recommendedcall，system will has/have default value.system will based on not  same's profileSets default parameter。

-   This interface must be set after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next I-frame.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_h264\_trans](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698005)interface，Gets currentencoding channel's transconfigure，then perform the configuration。

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface sets H.264protocolencoding channel entropyencoding's configure。
-   变换、量化属性主要由四个参数组成： 
    -   entropy\_coding\_mode\_i：Iframe entropyencodingmethod/mode ，entropy\_coding\_mode\_i = 0indicates Iframeuse cavlcencoding, entropy\_coding\_mode\_i = 1indicates Iframeuse cabacencodingmethod/mode 。
    -   entropy\_coding\_mode\_p：Pframe entropyencodingmethod/mode ，entropy\_coding\_mode\_p = 0indicates Pframeuse cavlcencoding, entropy\_coding\_mode\_p = 1indicates Pframeuse cabacencodingmethod/mode 。
    -   entropy\_coding\_mode\_b：Bframe entropyencodingmethod/mode ，entropy\_coding\_mode\_b = 0indicates Bframeuse cavlcencoding, entropy\_coding\_mode\_b = 1indicates Bframeuse cabacencodingmethod/mode 。
    -   cabac\_init\_idc：cabac初始化索引，系统默认为0。具体含义请参见H.264协议。

-   I/P/Bframe entropyencodingmethod/mode can respectively Sets 。
-   Baseline profilenot supportedcabacencodingmethod/mode ，not supportedBframe，supportscavlcencodingmethod/mode ，main profile、high profileand svc-tsupportscabacencodingmethod/mode and cavlcencodingmethod/mode 。
-   Cabac编码方式相对于cavlc编码方式，需要更大的计算量，可是，会产生更少的码流。如果系统性能不够富裕，建议用户可以采取I帧cavlc编码，P/B帧cabac编码。
-   this interfaceis an advanced interface，users may selectively call，not recommendedcall，system will has/have default value.system will based on not  same's profileSets default parameter。

**Table 1**  not  sameprofilebelow/under system default entropy codingparameter

-   This interface must be set after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next I-frame.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_h264\_entropy](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698297)interface，Gets currentencoding channel's Entropyconfigure，then perform the configuration。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface sets H.264protocolencoding channel's Deblocking's configure
-   transform 、 amount/quantity convertattributemainly by/from three  parameterGroup become: 
    -   disable\_deblocking\_filter\_idc: specific meaning refer to H.264protocol。
    -   slice\_alpha\_c0\_offset\_div2: specific meaning refer to H.264protocol。
    -   slice\_beta\_offset\_div2: specific meaning refer to H.264protocol。

-   system default open deblockingfunction，default disable\_deblocking\_filter\_idc = 0，slice\_alpha\_c0\_offset\_div2 = 0，slice\_beta\_offset\_div2 = 0。
-   如果用户想关闭deblocking功能，可以将disable\_deblocking\_filter\_idc置为1。
-   This interface must be configured after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.this interface When called during encoding,below/under one frame takes effect.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_h264\_dblk](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441657989)interface，Gets currentencoding channel's Dblkconfigure，then perform the configuration。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface sets JPEGprotocolencoding channel's advanced parameters
-   高级参数主要由五个参数组成
    -   qfactor：量化表因子范围为\[1, 99\]，qfactor越大，量化表中的量化系数越小，得到的图像质量会更好，同时，编码压缩率更低。同理qfactor越小，量化表中的量化系数越大，得到的图像质量会更差，同时，编码压缩率更高。具体的qfactor与量化表的关系请见RFC2435标准。
    -   y\_qt\[OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_QT\_COEF\_NUM\]，cb\_qt\[OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_QT\_COEF\_NUM\]，cr\_qt\[OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_QT\_COEF\_NUM\]: corresponding three  quantization tablespace ，user can via this three  parameterSets user 's quantization table.
    -   mcu\_per\_ecs: each Ecsin/within contains multiple/morefew/fewerMcu。system Modemcu\_per\_ecs = 0，indicates currentframe's all's MCUis encodingis/for one ECS。mcu\_per\_ecs's minimum  valueis/for 0，maximum  valuedoes not exceed \{\(picwidth+15\)\>\>4 \* \(picheight+15\)\>\>4 \* 2, 65535\}'s relatively small value.when mcu\_per\_ecsconfigurerelatively small  when, 容易引起CPUoccupied by/uses time relatively long , etc.phenomenon ，customer mustbased on actual use  need require合理configure。
    -   ecs\_output\_en: onlyHi3403V100/SS626V100supports，not supported's default  valueis/for TD\_FALSE。

-   如果用户想使用自己的量化表，在设置量化表的同时，请将qfactor设置为50。
-   This interface must be configured after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.this interface When called during encoding,below/under one frameencoding takes effect.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_jpeg\_param](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441698301)interface，Gets currentencoding channel's jpeg\_paramconfigure，then perform the configuration。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

-   rate controller's advanced parametersby/from below parameterGroup become: 

    -   threshold\_i\[OT\_VENC\_TEXTURE\_THRESHOLD\_SIZE  \]，threshold\_p\[OT\_VENC\_TEXTURE\_THRESHOLD\_SIZE\]，threshold\_b\[OT\_VENC\_TEXTURE\_THRESHOLD\_SIZE\]: respectively 衡 amount/quantityIframe，Pframe，Bframe's macro block complex  degree's one Group thresholdvalue.this Groupthreshold according to from small to large 's sequence/order in sequence排column ，each threshold 's the value range is \[0, 255\]。this Groupthreshold used for in/at perform macro block  levelbitratecontrol  when, based on imagecomplex  degreefor/to each macro block 's Qpperform  fitwhen 's  adjust whole/entire。for H.264/H.265protocol，macro block  levelbitratecontrol 既has/have  add square/methodto （maximum  add16），also has/have  reduce square/methodto （maximum  reduce16）， add reduce square/methodto 's control  retrieve/get determinein/at direction，假设direction=j, i.e., ifcurrentmacro block 's imagecomplex  degreeless than equal to threshold\[j-1\]threshold  when, currentmacro block 's Qp valuethen in/at macro block line startingQp value based on  reduce removex；ifcurrentmacro block 's imagecomplex  degreegreater than threshold\[j-1\]threshold  when, currentmacro block 's Qp valuethen in/at macro block line startingQp value based on  addon/above y。Cindicates imagecomplex  degree，x，y's value rangeas follows:C <threshold\[0\] when, x=j；threshold\[0\]≤C<threshold\[1\] when, x=j-1；threshold\[1\]≤C<threshold\[2\] when, x=j-2；threshold\[2\]≤C<threshold\[3\] when, x=j-3；in sequenceby analogy，threshold\[j-1\]≤C≤threshold\[j\] when, x=y=0；threshold\[j\] < C≤threshold\[j+1\] when, y=1; threshold\[j+1\] < C≤threshold\[j+2\] when, y=2；threshold\[j+2\] < C≤threshold\[j+3\] when, y=3；threshold\[j+3\] < C≤threshold\[j+4\] when, y=4；so as to this by analogy，threshold\[15\] < C when, y=16-j。

        H.264/H.265default  valueis/for : 

        threshold\_i\[0,0,0,0,3,3,5,5,8,8,8,15,15,20,25,25\]、threshold\_p\[0,0,0,0,3,3,5,5,8,8,8,15,15,20,25,25\]、threshold\_b\[0,0,0,0,3,3,5,5,8,8,8,15,15,20,25,25\]。

    -   row\_qp\_delta：在宏块级码率控制时，每一行宏块的起始Qp相对于帧起始Qp的波动幅度值。对于码率波动较严格的场景下，可以尝试将此参数调大，实现更加精确的码率控制，但可能会导致某些帧图像内部的图像质量有差异。在高码率时，该值推荐为0；中码率时推荐该值为0或1；低码率时推荐该值为2～5。
    -   first\_frame\_start\_qp：第一帧的起始Qp值，CBR/VBR/AVBR/QVBR/CVBR有效。first\_frame\_start\_qp 如果为-1则第一帧的起始QP由编码器内部计算，如果为其它合法值则由用户指定该合法值为第一帧起始QP。该值默认为-1。此处第一帧的含义是：通道创建，GopMode切换，RCMode切换，或分辨率切换后，序列的第一个IDR帧。需要注意如果第一帧编码完成后判定为重编，重编的帧不是第一帧，不受first\_frame\_start\_qp 的约束。
    -   direction：在宏块级码率控制时，用于控制减方向threshold的下标，例如当direction=8，C表示图像复杂度，表示从threshold\[0\]到threshold\[7\]为减方向的阈值，从threshold\[8\]到threshold\[15\]是加方向的阈值。
    -   scene\_chg\_detect: adaptivedetection currentencodingscenario whether occurs changes,enableafter will improve scenario switch  when's bitratefluctuation,VBR/AVBR/QVBR/CVBRhas/have effect.enablescenario switch detection  when canenableadaptiveinsert IDRframe，in/at scenario switch  whenencodingIDRframe。mustnoteclose re- encodeafter detection scenario switch will  losteffect.

    CBRmainly parameteras follows:

    -   min\_i\_proportion&max\_i\_proportion：CBR高级参数，分别表示的是最小IP比例和最大IP比例。IP比例表示I帧和P帧的Bits数的比例。这两个值用于钳位IP比例的范围。当min\_i\_proportion被调整较大时，会导致I帧清晰，P帧模糊。当max\_i\_proportion被调整较小时，会导致I帧模糊，P帧清晰。在正常情况下不建议对IP大小比进行约束，避免带来呼吸效应和码率波动，默认min\_i\_proportion设为1，max\_i\_proportion设为100。在对I帧大小有约束的场景时，可以根据对I帧大小波动的需求来设置min\_i\_proportion和max\_i\_proportion的值。
    -   max\_qp&min\_qp: CBRadvanced parameters，max\_qp、min\_qprepresents currentframe maximumQp and minimumQp。This clamp has the strongest effect; allother adjustments to imageQp adjustments, such as macroblock-level bitrate control，will ultimately be constrained to this maximumQp and minimumQp。min\_qp，max\_qpwithin part has/have default  value，in/at for/to quality Noneunder special requirements, recommendeddo not change this Groupparameter。
    -   max\_i\_qp&min\_i\_qp: CBRadvanced parameters，max\_i\_qp、min\_i\_qprepresents current sequencecolumn IDRframe maximumQp and minimumQp。This clamp has the strongest effect; allother adjustments to imageQp adjustments, such as macroblock-level bitrate control，will ultimately be constrained to this maximumQp and minimumQp。min\_i\_qp，max\_i\_qpwithin part has/have default  value，in/at for/to quality Noneunder special requirements, recommendeddo not change this Groupparameter。

    VBRmainly parameteras follows:

    -   chg\_pos：VBR高级参数，表示的是VBR开始调整Qp时码率与最大码率的比值。系统默认为90，如果在内容变化剧烈且要求不超出最大码率的场景，建议减小此值，同时增大delta\_qp，但码率控制稳定时的码率偏小和质量偏差。
    -   min\_i\_proportion&max\_i\_proportion：VBR高级参数，分别表示的是最小IP比例和最大IP比例。IP比例表示I帧和P帧的Bits数的比例。这两个值用于钳位IP比例的范围。当min\_i\_proportion被调整较大时，会导致I帧清晰，P帧模糊。当max\_i\_proportion被调整较小时，会导致I帧模糊，P帧清晰。在正常情况下不建议对IP大小比进行约束，避免带来呼吸效应和码率波动，默认min\_i\_proportion设为1，max\_i\_proportion设为100。在对I帧大小有约束的场景时，可以根据对I帧大小波动的需求来设置min\_i\_proportion和max\_i\_proportion的值。max\_qp&min\_qp：VBR高级参数，max\_qp、min\_qp表示的是当前帧的最大Qp和最小Qp。这个钳位效果最强烈，所有其他对图像Qp的调整，如宏块级码率控制，最终都会被约束到这个最大Qp和最小Qp。默认值min\_qp为16，max\_qp为51在对质量None特殊需求下，建议不更改此组参数。
    -   max\_i\_qp&min\_i\_qp: VBRadvanced parameters，max\_i\_qp、min\_i\_qprepresents current sequencecolumn IDRframe maximumQp and minimumQp。This clamp has the strongest effect; allother adjustments to imageQp adjustments, such as macroblock-level bitrate control，will ultimately be constrained to this maximumQp and minimumQp。default  valuemin\_i\_qpis/for 16，max\_i\_qpis/for 51。in/at for/to quality Noneunder special requirements, recommendeddo not change this Groupparameter。

    AVBRmainly parameteras follows:

    -   chg\_pos：AVBR高级参数，表示的是AVBR开始调整Qp时码率与最大码率的比值。系统默认为90，如果在内容变化剧烈且要求不超出最大码率的场景，建议减小此值，同时增大delta\_qp，但码率控制稳定时的码率偏小和质量偏差。
    -   min\_i\_proportion&max\_i\_proportion：AVBR高级参数，分别表示的是最小IP比例和最大IP比例。IP比例表示I帧和P帧的Bits数的比例。这两个值用于钳位IP比例的范围。当min\_i\_proportion被调整较大时，会导致I帧清晰，P帧模糊。当max\_i\_proportion被调整较小时，会导致I帧模糊，P帧清晰。在正常情况下不建议对IP大小比进行约束，避免带来呼吸效应和码率波动，默认min\_i\_proportion设为1，max\_i\_proportion设为100。在对I帧大小有约束的场景时，可以根据对I帧大小波动的需求来设置min\_i\_proportion和max\_i\_proportion的值。
    -   max\_qp&min\_qp: AVBRadvanced parameters，max\_qp、min\_qprepresents currentframe maximumQp and minimumQp。This clamp has the strongest effect; allother adjustments to imageQp adjustments, such as macroblock-level bitrate control，will ultimately be constrained to this maximumQp and minimumQp。min\_qpand max\_qpwithin part has/have default  value，in/at for/to quality Noneunder special requirements, recommendeddo not change this Groupparameter。
    -   max\_i\_qp&min\_i\_qp: AVBRadvanced parameters，max\_i\_qp、min\_i\_qprepresents current sequencecolumn IDRframe maximumQp and minimumQp。This clamp has the strongest effect; allother adjustments to imageQp adjustments, such as macroblock-level bitrate control，will ultimately be constrained to this maximumQp and minimumQp。default  valuemin\_i\_qpis/for 16，max\_i\_qpis/for 51。min\_qpand max\_qpwithin part has/have default  value，in/at for/to quality Noneunder special requirements, recommendeddo not change this Groupparameter。
    -   max\_still\_qp: 静止scenario QP maximum value，target bitratereduce past/too large will 带 to静止scenario QP升height ，imagequality 变 difference，at this time can use this  变 amount/quantitylimit 静止scenario QP maximumvalue.
    -   motion\_sensitivity: motion 敏 sense degree，the/this  valuethe more large indicates bitratecontrol for/to motion 's changes反 applymore 快，but simultaneously for/to 噪声also more 敏 sense。
    -   min\_qp\_delta: 帧级QP最小值和CU级QP最小值的差值，在图像内容比较简单时，码控会降低Qp值，当帧级Qp调节到最小值时，帧级码率控制就不再下调Qp，但是CU/宏块级码率控制依然生效，图像中平坦区域Qp能够下调到更小值。

    QVBRmainly parameteras follows:

    -   min\_i\_proportion&max\_i\_proportion：QVBR高级参数，分别表示的是最小IP比例和最大IP比例。IP比例表示I帧和P帧的Bits数的比例。这两个值用于钳位IP比例的范围。当min\_i\_proportion被调整较大时，会导致I帧清晰，P帧模糊。当max\_i\_proportion被调整较小时，会导致I帧模糊，P帧清晰。在正常情况下不建议对IP大小比进行约束，避免带来呼吸效应和码率波动，默认min\_i\_proportion设为1，max\_i\_proportion设为100。在对I帧大小有约束的场景时，可以根据对I帧大小波动的需求来设置min\_i\_proportion和max\_i\_proportion的值。
    -   max\_qp&min\_qp: QVBRadvanced parameters，max\_qp、min\_qprepresents currentframe maximumQp and minimumQp。This clamp has the strongest effect; allother adjustments to imageQp adjustments, such as macroblock-level bitrate control，will ultimately be constrained to this maximumQp and minimumQp。default  valuemin\_qpis/for 16，max\_qpis/for 51in/at for/to quality Noneunder special requirements, recommendeddo not change this Groupparameter。
    -   max\_i\_qp&min\_i\_qp: QVBRadvanced parameters，max\_i\_qp、min\_i\_qprepresents current sequencecolumn IDRframe maximumQp and minimumQp。This clamp has the strongest effect; allother adjustments to imageQp adjustments, such as macroblock-level bitrate control，will ultimately be constrained to this maximumQp and minimumQp。default  valuemin\_i\_qpis/for 16，max\_i\_qpis/for 51。in/at for/to quality Noneunder special requirements, recommendeddo not change this Groupparameter。
    -   max\_bit\_percent & min\_bit\_percent : QVBRadvanced parameters，target bitrateon/above 浮and below/under 浮's percentage 。when scenario complex ，encodingquality below/under  reduce/lower when, encoderwill in/at set/configure 's percentage within on/above 浮target bitrate，ensure imagequality ；in/at scenario 简single-，encodingquality 升height  when, encoderwill in/at set/configure 's percentage within below/under 浮target bitrate，save bitrate。
    -   max\_psnr\_fluctuate & min\_psnr\_fluctuate : QVBRadvanced parameters，encodingquality PSNR's  region inter-。PSNRthe more height  generation/represent tablequality the more good ，PSNRthe more low  generation/represent tablequality the more  difference。

    CVBRmainly parameteras follows:

    -   min\_i\_proportion&max\_i\_proportion：CVBR高级参数，分别表示的是最小IP比例和最大IP比例。IP比例表示I帧和P帧的Bits数的比例。这两个值用于钳位IP比例的范围。

        when min\_i\_proportionadjusted larger when, it will causes Iframesharp/clear,Pframeblurry.

        when max\_i\_proportionadjusted smaller when, it will causes Iframeblurry,Pframesharp/clear。

        in/at  positive/correct often/usualcondition/casebelow/under not recommendedfor/to IPsize  compareperform constraint ，to avoid breathing effectand bitratefluctuation,default min\_i\_proportionset to 1，max\_i\_proportionset to 100。in/at for/to Iframesize has/have constraint 's scenario  when, can based on for/to Iframesize fluctuation requirements toSets min\_i\_proportionand max\_i\_proportion's value.

    -   max\_qp&min\_qp: CVBRadvanced parameters，max\_qp、min\_qprepresents currentframe maximumQp and minimumQp。This clamp has the strongest effect; allother adjustments to imageQp adjustments, such as macroblock-level bitrate control，will ultimately be constrained to this maximumQp and minimumQp。default  valuemin\_qpis/for 22，max\_qpis/for 47in/at for/to quality Noneunder special requirements, recommendeddo not change this Groupparameter。
    -   max\_i\_qp&min\_i\_qp: CVBRadvanced parameters，max\_i\_qp、min\_i\_qprepresents current sequencecolumn IDRframe maximumQp and minimumQp。This clamp has the strongest effect; allother adjustments to imageQp adjustments, such as macroblock-level bitrate control，will ultimately be constrained to this maximumQp and minimumQp。default  valuemin\_i\_qpis/for 20，max\_i\_qpis/for 47。in/at for/to quality Noneunder special requirements, recommendeddo not change this Groupparameter。
    -   min\_qp\_delta: CVBR高级参数，帧级QP最小值和CU级QP最小值的差值，在图像内容比较简单时，码控会降低Qp值，当帧级Qp调节到最小值时，帧级码率控制就不再下调Qp，但是CU/宏块级码率控制依然生效，图像中平坦区域Qp能够下调到更小值。
    -   max\_qp\_delta: CVBR高级参数，帧级QP最大值和CU级QP最大值的差值，在图像内容比较复杂时，码控会增大Qp值，当帧级Qp调节到最大值时，帧级码率控制就不再上调Qp，但是CU/宏块级码率控制依然生效，图像中强纹理区域Qp能够上升到更大值。

-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   VPSS-VENC处于卷绕Low Latency或单帧Low LatencyMode时，不支持码率控制重编功能，请客户设置最大重编次数为0，否则会导致通道频繁丢帧。SS528V100、SS625V100、SS524V100、SS522V101、SS626V100不支持Low Latency，仅Hi3403V100支持非卷绕Low Latency。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Note**

-   this interfaceused for Sets the snapshot mode for the JPEG snapshot channel.
-   This interface must be called after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.
-   this interfaceonlyfor/to JPEGType's encoding channelhas/have effect.
-   通道抓拍Mode有两种： 
    -   OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_ENC\_ALLMode: channelstartreceiveimage after, encodingallreceive's image；
    -   OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_ENC\_SNAP Mode：通道启动接收图像之后，只编码被标记为抓拍帧的图像。

-   创建JPEG通道之后，JPEG通道默认处于OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_ENC\_ALLMode。当用户需要只抓拍被标记的图像时，可以调用本接口，设置JPEG通道为OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_ENC\_SNAP Mode。
-   SS528V100/SS625V100/SS524V100/SS522V101/Hi3403V100/SS626V100Solutionon/above ，corresponding bind's VPSS channelsupportscompressionand  non-compressionformatOutput。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Requirements**

Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h

Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface must be called after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.
-   This interface sets H.264/H.265protocolencoding channelstream's  sub- splitmethod/mode 。
-   Slice sub- splitattributemainly by/from below parameterdetermine 

    -   enable: currentframewhether perform slice sub- split。
    -   split\_mode: slice sub- splitMode，split\_mode = 0indicates according to byte number toperform  sub- split，split\_mode = 1indicates according to macro block line or  LCUline perform  sub- split。split\_mode \>=2not is supports。

    split\_mode必须在enable为真的条件下有效，否则，None效。

    -   slice\_size: slice's size ，when split\_modenot  same when, slice\_sizeindicates not  same's 意义。when split\_mode = 0 when, slice\_sizeindicates 平all/each each slice's byte number's multiple/morefew/fewer，encoderfrom image's  lefton/above corner start ，from  left to/until右，from on/above  to/until under,  press/by光栅sequence/order  press/bymacro block perform encoding。each slicesize according to slice\_sizeperform  divide sub-。most 终encodingslicesize not one 定with/and user Sets 's slice\_size严 format/grid mutual, etc.,may exist 误 difference。when encoding to/untilimage's most after one slice when, 剩余's macro block byte numbernot  sufficientslice\_size，剩余's allmacro block is encoding becomeone slice。when split\_mode = 1 when, slice\_sizeindicates each slice占imagemacro block line  numberor LCUline  number，slice\_size's single-bit is/for macro block line or  LCUline  number。 andwhen encoding to/untilimage's most after 几line ，not  sufficientslice\_size when, 剩余's macro block line or  LCUline is  divide sub-is/for one slice。
    -   slice\_output\_en: onlyHi3403V100/SS626V100/SS524V100/SS528V100supports，not supported's default  valueis/for TD\_FALSE，when slice\_output\_enis/for TD\_TRUE when, channelattributeis\_by\_framemustis/for TD\_FALSE，when slice\_output\_enis/for TD\_TRUE whenonlysupportssplit\_mode = 1's Mode。

-   当按字节划分slice时，若客户配置的slice\_size较小，会存在一帧被分割成较多slice的情况。此时会导致码流压缩率较低和CPU占用时间较长等现象，客户需要结合实际需求合理配置。
-   channelencodingwidth  degreeless than 256 when, not supported press/bybyte  divide sub-slice。
-   this interfaceis an advanced interface，users may selectively call，recommendednot call，system default enableis/for false。
-   This interface must be configured after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_slice\_split](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098666)interface，Gets currentchannel's slicesplitconfigure，then perform the configuration。
-   this interfaceonlysupportsH.264/H.265channel。

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface sets H.265protocolencoding channelstream's prediction unit PU's configure。
-   PUattributemainly by/from two parameterdetermine : 
    -   constrained\_intra\_pred\_flag: for the specific meaning, refer to H.265protocol。
    -   strong\_intra\_smoothing\_enabled\_flag: for the specific meaning, refer to H.265protocol。

-   This is an advanced interface; users may call it selectively. It is not recommended to call it; the system has default values.
-   This interface must be configured after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.this interface When called during encoding,below/under one Iframe takes effect.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_h265\_pu](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098806)interface，Gets currentchannel's PU's configure，then perform the configuration。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Requirements**

Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h

Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface sets H.265protocolencoding channelstream's transform  amount/quantity convert's configure。
-   Transattributemainly by/from below parameterdetermine 
    -   cb\_qp\_offset：具体的含义，请参见H.265协议关于pps\_cb\_qp\_offset的解释。
    -   cr\_qp\_offset：具体的含义，请参见H.265协议关于pps\_cr\_qp\_offset的解释。
    -   scaling\_list\_en: indicates quantization tablewhether valid/effective ，Noneset to when using quantization table0。
    -   scaling\_list\_tu4\_valid: indicates inter\_scaling\_list4x4、intra\_scaling\_list4x4quantization tablewhether valid/effective ，use protocoldefault set to when using quantization table0。
    -   inter\_scaling\_list4x4\[2\]\[16\]: inter\_scaling\_list4x4\[0\]\[16\]indicates frame inter-brightnessquantization table,inter\_scaling\_list4x4\[1\]\[16\]indicates frame inter- color degreequantization table, canby/from user via this  numberGroupprovides quantization table.
    -   intra\_scaling\_list4x4\[2\]\[16\]: intra\_scaling\_list4x4\[0\]\[16\]indicates framewithin brightnessquantization table,intra\_scaling\_list4x4\[1\]\[16\]indicates framewithin  color degreequantization table, canby/from user via this  numberGroupprovides quantization table.
    -   scaling\_list\_tu8\_valid: indicates inter\_scaling\_list8x8、intra\_scaling\_list8x8quantization tablewhether valid/effective ，use protocoldefault set to when using quantization table0。
    -   inter\_scaling\_list8x8\[2\]\[64\]: inter\_scaling\_list8x8\[0\]\[ 64\]indicates frame inter-brightnessquantization table,inter\_scaling\_list8x8\[1\]\[ 64\]indicates frame inter- color degreequantization table, canby/from user via this  numberGroupprovides quantization table.
    -   intra\_scaling\_list8x8\[2\]\[64\]: intra\_scaling\_list8x8\[0\]\[64\]indicates framewithin brightnessquantization table,intra\_scaling\_list8x8\[1\]\[64\]indicates framewithin  color degreequantization table, canby/from user via this  numberGroupprovides quantization table.
    -   scaling\_list\_tu16\_valid: indicates inter\_scaling\_list16x16、intra\_scaling\_list16x16quantization tablewhether valid/effective ，use protocoldefault set to when using quantization table0。
    -   inter\_scaling\_list16x16\[2\]\[64\]: inter\_scaling\_list16x16\[0\]\[ 64\]indicates frame inter-brightnessquantization table,Inter\_scaling\_list16x16\[1\]\[ 64\]indicates frame inter- color degreequantization table, canby/from user via this  numberGroupprovides quantization table.
    -   intra\_scaling\_list16x16\[2\]\[64\]: intra\_scaling\_list16x16\[0\]\[64\]indicates framewithin brightnessquantization table,intra\_scaling\_list16x16 \[1\]\[64\]indicates framewithin  color degreequantization table, canby/from user via this  numberGroupprovides quantization table.
    -   scaling\_list\_tu32\_valid: indicates inter\_scaling\_list32x32、intra\_scaling\_list32x32quantization tablewhether valid/effective ，use protocoldefault set to when using quantization table0。
    -   inter\_scaling\_list32x32\[64\]: inter\_scaling\_list32x32\[ 64\]indicates frame inter-brightnessquantization table, canby/from user via this  numberGroupprovides quantization table.
    -   intra\_scaling\_list32x32\[64\]: intra\_scaling\_list32x32\[64\]indicates framewithin brightnessquantization table, canby/from user via this  numberGroupprovides quantization table.
    -   This is an advanced interface; users may call it selectively. It is not recommended to call it; the system has default values.

-   This interface must be configured after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.this interface When called during encoding,below/under one Iframe takes effect.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.

-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_h265\_trans](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658473)interface，Gets currentchannel's transform  amount/quantity convert's configure，then perform the configuration。
-   SS528V100/SS625V100/SS524V100/SS522V101/Hi3403V100/SS626V100onlysupportsNonequantization table.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="50%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.2 "><p id="p17641mcpsimp"><a name="p17641mcpsimp"></a><a name="p17641mcpsimp"></a>Success.</p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr id="row17642mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="50%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.1 "><p id="p17644mcpsimp"><a name="p17644mcpsimp"></a><a name="p17644mcpsimp"></a>Non-0</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="50%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.2 "><p id="p17646mcpsimp"><a name="p17646mcpsimp"></a><a name="p17646mcpsimp"></a>On failure, returns an error code.</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   本接口用于设置H.265协议编码通道的Deblocking属性和边界滤波属性。
-   Deblockingattributemainly by/from three  parameterdetermine 
    -   slice\_deblocking\_filter\_disabled\_flag: for the specific meaning, refer to H.265protocol。
    -   slice\_beta\_offset\_div2: for the specific meaning, refer to H.265protocol。
    -   slice\_tc\_offset\_div2: for the specific meaning, refer to H.265protocol。

-   边界滤波attributemainly by/from 二 parameterdetermine 
    -   loop\_filter\_across\_tiles\_enabled\_flag: for the specific meaning, refer to H.265protocol。
    -   loop\_filter\_across\_slices\_enabled\_flag: for the specific meaning, refer to H.265protocol。

-   本接口属于高级接口，用户可以选择性调用，建议不调用，系统默认打开deblocking功能和slice, tile边界滤波功能，默认slice\_deblocking\_filter\_disabled\_flag = 0，slice\_tc\_offset\_div2 = 0，slice\_beta\_offset\_div2 = 0，loop\_filter\_across\_tiles\_enabled\_flag=1，loop\_filter\_across\_slices\_enabled\_flag=1。
-   如果用户想关闭deblocking功能，可以将slice\_deblocking\_filter\_disabled\_flag置为1。
-   如果使能slice与tile边界滤波功能，在划分slice和tile时生效。
-   本接口在编码通道创建之后，编码通道销毁之前设置。此接口在编码过程中被调用时，Deblocking属性等到下一帧时生效，边界滤波属性等到下一个I帧生效。
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_h265\_dblk](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658097)interface，Gets currentchannel's configure，then perform the configuration。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Note**

-   This interface sets H.265protocolencoding channelstream's Saoconfigure。
-   Saoattributemainly by/from two parameterdetermine 
    -   slice\_sao\_luma\_flag：当前slice亮度分量是否作Sao滤波。
    -   slice\_sao\_chroma\_flag：当前slice色度分量是否作Sao滤波。

-   this interfaceis an advanced interface，users may selectively call，recommendednot call，system default open saofunction，default slice\_sao\_luma\_flag = 1，slice\_sao\_chroma\_flag = 1。
-   This interface must be configured after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_h265\_sao](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658145)interface，Gets currentchannel's Saoconfigure，then perform the configuration。

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Requirements**

**Note**

-   如果lost\_param为空，则返回Failure。
-   lost\_param主要由四个参数决定 
    -   enable: discard frame开关
    -   bit\_rate\_threshold: discard framethreshold 
    -   frame\_gap：最大允许连续丢帧帧数。
    -   mode: discard framestrategy Mode

-   this interfaceis an advanced interface，users may selectively call，system has/have default  value，default in/at  instantaneous whenbitrateexceeds threshold  whenis/for discard frame。
-   this interfaceprovides  instantaneous whenbitrateexceeds threshold  whentwo  typeprocessing method/mode : discard frameand encodingp\_skipframe，Mjpegonlysupportsdiscard frameprocessing method/mode 。
-   frame\_gap控制是否均匀丢帧或均匀编码p\_skip帧。如[图1](#fig9874105285115)所示。

**图 1**  丢帧示意图<a name="fig9874105285115"></a>  
![](figures/丢帧示意图.png "丢帧示意图")
-   This interface must be configured after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.ifin/at gopModeswitch after callthe/this interface， needwait until GOPtakes effect after the/this interface generateeffect.
-   p\_skipframeonlysupportsGOPModeis/for OT\_VENC\_GOP\_MODE\_NORMAL\_P's encoding channel。
-   切换GOPMode后丢帧开关会默认关闭，如需继续使用该接口需要重新配置。
-   such as/if currentframeosdenable andosdhas/have more new ，cannot encodingis/for p\_skipframe。
-   encodingp\_skipframewith/and Pframe refreshIslicefunctionare mutually exclusive.
-   当设置H.265协议通道编码p\_skip帧时，本模块内部会关闭SAO滤波，故建议客户调用[ss\_mpi\_venc\_set\_h265\_sao](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658333)关闭SAO功能。
-   ifframe\_gapequal to 0 when, indicates  instantaneous whenbitrateexceeds threshold allow one  directlydiscard frame， directlyto  instantaneous whenbitratenot greater than  thresholdvalue.
-   enable skipframereference or  svc-tencoding，base layerframecannot encodingis/for p\_skipframe。
-   this interfaceonlysupportsH.264/H.265/Mjpeg 's CBR/VBRbitratecontrol Mode、H.264/H.265 's AVBR/QVBR/CVBRbitratecontrol Mode's parameter's Sets 。

**Example**

**Related topics**

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface sets H.264/H.265 protocolencoding channelSSE  region'sparameter。
-   SSE parametermainly by/from three  parameterdetermine 。
    -   idx：系统支持每个通道可设置8 个SSE 区域，系统内部按照0～7 的索引号对SSE 区域进行管理，idx 表示的用户设置SSE的索引号。SSE 区域之间可以互相叠加，且当发生叠加时，SSE区域之间按照自己对应区域上报SSE总和，互不影响。
    -   enable: specified current's SSEregion whether enable。
    -   rect: specified current's SSE  region'sbit  setCoordinatesand  region'ssize 。

        H.264's SSE region'sstarting pointCoordinatesmustin/at imagerange  within, and must16alignment；SSE region'slong width mustis 16alignment， andregion mustin/at imagerange within 。

        H.265's SSE region'sstarting pointCoordinatesmustin/at imagerange  within, and mustLCUalignment；SSE region'slong width mustis LCUalignment， andregion mustin/at imagerange within 。

-   本接口属于高级接口，系统默认八个SSE区域使能，不调用此接口时，默认均为第一个宏块区域。用户可以调用此接口改变SSE区域，从而在水印信息中得到每个区域的SSE总和。
-   This interface must be configured after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.this interfacein/at encoding when called during encoding，wait for below/under one frame whenoccurs 。
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_sse\_rgn](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658541)interface，Gets currentchannel's SSEconfigure，then perform the configuration。
-   仅Hi3403V100的H.264编码通道支持该接口，H.265全支持。

**Example**

None

**Related topics**

None.

-   This interface gets H.264/H.265protocolencoding channel's index's SSE configure。
-   This interface must be called after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.

**Note**

-   This interface sets VENCchannelparameter。
-   VENC高级参数主要由六个参数决定。
    -   color\_to\_grey\_en：开启或关闭一个通道的彩转灰功能，默认为关闭。在编码过程中被调用时，等到下一个I帧编码时生效。
    -   priority: Sets encoding channelpriority ，default is/for 0
    -   max\_stream\_cnt: used for Sets encoding channel's streambufferin/within  capability enough/sufficientbuffer maximumstreamframe number

        若缓存码流帧数已达到最大码流帧数，当前待编码图像因码流buffer满而不被编码。

        maximum streamframe numberin/at createchannel whenby/from system within part specified default  value，default  valueis/for 200。

        in/at createchannel after, destroyencoding channelbefore all/each can is Sets 。in/at below/under one framestart encodingbefore  generateeffect.this parameterallow is multiple times Sets ，recommendedin/at createencoding channelSuccess after, startencodingbefore perform Sets ，not recommendedin/at encodingduring the processdynamic  adjust whole/entire。

    -   poll\_wake\_up\_frame\_cnt：当通道使用超时或阻塞获取码流，编码指定的帧之后唤醒阻塞接口。

        默认一帧唤醒一次。在编码过程中被设置时，等待下一帧时生效。阻塞获取码流时，建议poll\_wake\_up\_frame\_cnt应小于max\_stream\_cnt。

    -   crop\_info: used for Sets channel's cropattribute。channelwill  better/optimalfirst perform imagecrop， then/naturallyafter 基in/at cropafter 's image尺寸，with/and encoding channel's 尺寸perform  comparerelatively ，determine whether perform downscale 。
    -   frame\_rate：Sets channelframe ratecontrol attribute

        channelframe ratecontrol attributeincluding Inputframe ratesrc\_frame\_rateand Outputframe ratedst\_frame\_ratetwo some/partial 。

        Sets channelframe ratecontrol attribute when, Inputframe ratesrc\_frame\_rateand Outputframe ratedst\_frame\_ratemustsimultaneously greater than 0or simultaneously equal to -1。

        when Inputframe ratesrc\_frame\_rateand Outputframe ratedst\_frame\_rateall/each equal to -1 when, this  whenindicates not perform frame ratecontrol 。

        当Input帧率src\_frame\_rate小于Output帧率dst\_frame\_rate时为增帧Mode，以dst\_frame\_rate作为绝对Output帧率。例如，src\_frame\_rate<dst\_frame\_rate，dst\_frame\_rate为30时，表示编码最终会Output30帧。

        > **Note:** 
        >增帧Mode下，主要适用于稳定Output帧率的场景，当Input端断流时需要stop venc，否则断流之后会重复编最后一帧以达到目标帧率。

        when Inputframe ratesrc\_frame\_rategreater than or  equal to Outputframe ratedst\_frame\_rate whenis/for discard frame（ reduceframe）Mode， retrieve/getInput's some/partial frameperform encoding。 examplesuch as/if ，src\_frame\_rate = 30，dst\_frame\_rate = 15 when, indicates each Input30frameimage，only retrieve/getits/their  in 15frameperform encoding。

        丢帧（减帧）Mode下，当前端为固定频率的源时，例如VI，通道的帧率控制则准确；当前端为不固定频率的源时，例如VDEC，通道的帧率控制则不准确。

        JPEGnot supported increaseframeMode，Inputframe ratesrc\_frame\_ratemustgreater than or  equal to Outputframe ratedst\_frame\_rate。

        VPSS-VENC处于卷绕Low Latency或单bufferLow LatencyMode时，不支持增帧Mode，强制配置会导致通道频繁丢帧。SS528V100、SS625V100、SS524V100、SS522V101不支持VPSS-VENCLow Latency。

        JPEGE编码通道使能OT\_VENC\_PIC\_RECV\_MULTIMode时，建议在Input源（例如vpss）的一个通道进行帧率控制，编码会自动丢弃其他通道不需要编码的图像。如果Input源进行了帧率控制，不应再在编码通道进行帧率控制，可能会导致丢帧。

-   Sets has not been create's channel's parameter，then returns Failure。
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_chn\_param](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099214)interface，Gets currentchannel's parameterconfigure，then perform the configuration。
-   This interface must be configured after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.

None.

**Related topics**

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   本接口用于设置通道图像前景保护的配置。
-   This interface must be set after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   建议用户在调用此接口之前，先调用[ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_fg\_protect](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258646)接口，获取当前编码通道的前景保护的配置，然后再进行设置。

**Note**

-   The channel must be created; otherwise an error code indicating the channel is not created is returned.
-   绑定VB池和创建编码通道的操作，需要在同一个进程中执行。
-   user mustcallinterfacess\_mpi\_vb\_create\_pool（refer to ”System Control”chapter ）createone video bufferVB pool，again/then by calling interfacess\_mpi\_venc\_attach\_vb\_poolset currentencoding channelbindto fixed PoolId's VB poolin/within 。can set multiple encoding channelbindto the same VB poolin/within ，however cannot set the same encoding channelbindto multiple VB poolin/within 。
-   when  requireswitch currentencoding channelbind's VB pool when, only needagain/then  adjustonce interfacess\_mpi\_venc\_attach\_vb\_poolcorrect configuremustbindto 's VB poolis sufficient。
    1.   non-framesave Modebelow/under : 

        interfacewill not determine VB pool BlkSize and  BlkCnt's valid - property。encodingduring the processif VB poolin/within BlkSizeor BlkCnt not satisfy  need require，will causes encoderblocking 。

    2.  framesave Modebelow/under : 

        接口会判断存储Picture的VB池BlkSize 和 BlkCnt的合法性，若不满足需求，返回Failure，内部继续使用已绑定的VB池进行编码；若满足需求，返回Success，内部切换到新池子编码，动态切换VB池会产生一个I帧。

-   onlyhas/have H.264/H.265encodingsupportsUserVB poolmethod/mode ，ifcurrentencodingframe bufferallocate method/mode use 's not is encodingUserVB pool，then returns channelnot supported's Error Code。
-   poolmustis already createVB pool's valid/effective PoolId，contains storage Picture's VB pooland storage Pictureinformation 's VB pool。
-   帧节省Mode下使用UserVB方式，调用接口ss\_mpi\_venc\_attach\_vb\_pool时内部会判断存储Picture的VB池的BlkCnt 和 BlkSize是否符合本通道的编码需求（存储Picture信息的VB池的BlkCnt 和 BlkSize不会提前判断）。如果满足需求则接口返回Success，存储Picture的VB块即可被占用；否则接口返回Failure，Error CodeOT\_ERR\_VENC\_SIZE\_NOT\_ENOUGH表示绑定的Picture VB池BlkSize 不足，Error CodeOT\_ERR\_VENC\_NO\_MEM表示绑定的Picture VB池 BlkCnt 不足。

    -   intra32\_cost/intra16\_clost/intra8\_cost/intra4\_cost/inter64\_cost/inter32\_cost/inter16\_cost/inter8\_cost分别设置各自Mode的倾向性大小。默认每个cost值为8，即不做倾向性配置，每个Mode的cost设置越大意味着选择该Mode的倾向性越小。

-   Only H.265/H.264 encoding channels are supported.H.264encoding channelrecommended use High profile。

None.

None.

**Note**

-   The channel must be created; otherwise an error code indicating the channel is not created is returned.
-   本接口用于调试通道cu/mb选择SkipMode的倾向性，支持背景和前景的倾向性单独配置。
-   This interface must be set after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_skip\_bias](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098726)interface，Gets cu/mbselect SkipMode tendencyconfigure，then perform the configuration。
-   该功能主要由如下五个参数决定。
    -   enable: skiptendencyenablecontrol 。
    -   gainand offsetis before scene detection 's control parameter，used for generate before scene detection 's  thresholdvalue.threshold calculate as follows:\(\(gain \* madi + 8\) \>\> 4\) + offset，where madiis/for currentblock pixel 's 平all/each absolute误 difference\(first calculate currentblock pixel 's all/each  value， then/naturallyafter calculate currentblock allpixel with/and the/this all/each  valueabsolute difference's all/each  value\)。motion detection 's method : 利 usemotion 估计结果残 difference's SAD， followthis  threshold perform  comparerelatively ，if greater than this  threshold then 认is/for is before scene some/partial 。therefore for the same scenario ，threshold the more small ，detection  output/out tobefore scene region the more large ，反之亦 then/naturally。
    -   bg\_cost/fg\_costrespectively Sets background scene and before scene 's skiptendencysize 。default not adjust tendency, i.e., cost valueis/for 10。

-   Only H.265/H.264 encoding channels are supported.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

</td>

</tbody>
</table>

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h, ot\_comm\_vb.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   onlyH.264/H.265supportsde-breathing effectfunction。
-   The channel must be created; otherwise an error code indicating the channel is not created is returned.
-   This interface must be set after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   when GOPequal to 1 when, is not allowedenable removeexcept/divide bybreathing effectfunction。
-   ifenablePframe refreshI slice， needclosePframe refreshI slicefunction，again/then enable removeexcept/divide bybreathing effect，otherwisereturns is not allowed。
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_debreath\_effect](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258450)interface，Gets  removeexcept/divide bybreathing effectparameter，then perform the configuration。
-   使能该接口编码器会对图像做处理，有一定的性能损失，建议用户按需开启，默认为关闭。
-   VPSS-VENC placein/at wrap-around Low Latencyor single-frameLow LatencyMode when, not supportedde-breathing effectfunction。ifuser simultaneously enableLow Latencyand de-breathing effectfunction，de-breathing effectfunctionNoneeffect.SS528V100/SS625V100/SS524V100/SS522V101/SS626V100not supportedLow Latency，Hi3403V100supports non-wrap-around Low Latency。
-   supportssimultaneously enableframesave Modeand de-breathing effect。
-   the/this functionmainly by/from as followsthree  parameterdetermine 。
    -   enable:  removeexcept/divide bybreathing effectenablecontrol 。
    -   strength0为设置去除呼吸效应强度调节参数0，默认值为6。strength0值越大，呼吸效应改善会越弱，值越小，呼吸效应改善越明显，但是在编码压力很大且码率很低时，会导致图像更加模糊。
    -   strength1为设置去除呼吸效应强度调节参数1，默认值为16。其值越大，I帧会越大，其值越小，I帧会越小。如果I帧变大对客户应用有影响，客户可以尝试把strength1调小一点，会降低I帧码流的大小， 但是可能会减弱呼吸效应的改善程度。

-   原则上，对不同的场景，呼吸效应的改善要做到最佳，需要调节不同的参数。但是考虑到多场景的适应性，建议客户使用默认值。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h, ot\_comm\_vb.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   The channel must be created; otherwise an error code indicating the channel is not created is returned.
-   This interface must be configured after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.
-   this interface When called during encoding,below/under one Iframe takes effect.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_hierarchical\_qp](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099118)interface，then perform the configuration。
-   onlyin/at GOPTypeis/for NORMAL\_P、SMART\_P、ADV\_SMART\_P whensupportsthe/this interface。
-   Only H.265/H.264 encoding channels are supported.
-   recommendedin/at height frame ratemotion scenario  whenuse the/this function, i.e., SceneModeSets is/for OT\_VENC\_SCENE\_1。
-   recommendedin/at GOPModeis/for OT\_VENC\_GOP\_MODE\_NORMAL\_Pand OT\_VENC\_GOP\_MODE\_SMART\_Pand OT\_VENC\_GOP\_MODE\_ADV\_SMART\_P whenuse the/this function。
-   the/this functionmainly by/from as followsparameterdetermine 。
    -   enable:  sub- layerqpfunctionenableconfigure。
    -   qp\_delta：设置每一层帧相对于第0层p帧的qp\_delta。由于0层p帧的qp不需要进行调整，故qp\_delta\[0\]为第1层，qp\_delta\[1\]为第2层，以此类推。
    -   frame\_num: Sets each one  layerframe's count ，where frame\_num \[0\]is/for the 1 layer，frame\_num\[1\]is/for the 2 layer，so as to this by analogy。

-   分层QP示意图如[图1](#fig152253434317)和[图2](#fig16586145618411)所示。

**图 1**  normal\_p分层QP示意图<a name="fig152253434317"></a>

![](figures/smart_p-adv_smart_pp分层QP示意图.png "smart_p-adv_smart_pp分层QP示意图")

**Related topics**

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface sets JPEGand MJPEGencoding channelROIattribute。
-   ROIparameter：
    -   idx: supportseach channel canSets 16 ROI，encoderaccording to 0～15's index numberROIperform management，idxindicates 's user Sets ROI's index number.ROIbetween can overlay each other, andwhen occurs overlay when, ROIbetween 's priority according to index number0～15provided in orderheight 。
    -   enable: specified current's ROIwhether enable。
    -   level：指定当前的ROI采用的降码率等级，0\~3依次提高，等级越高降码率相对越多，图像质量损失也相对越多。
    -   rect: specified current's ROI's startingCoordinatesand  region'ssize 。ROI's starting pointCoordinatesmustin/at imagerange  within, and must16alignment；ROI region'slong width mustis 16alignmentand greater than 0；ROIregion mustin/at imagerange within 。

-   this interfaceis an advanced interface，default not enableROI，user mustcallthis interfaceenableROI。
-   This interface must be set after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.
-    non-ROIregion leveldefault is/for 2。
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to, before calling this interface,，first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_jpeg\_roi\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098586)interface，Gets currentchannel's ROIconfigure，then perform the configuration。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface must be called after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.
-   For H.264 encoding channels, setting SPS and PPS parameter set IDs is supported. If two PPSs exist, the second PPS ID is the set value plus 1.
-   For H.265 encoding channels, setting VPS, SPS, and PPS parameter set IDs is supported.
-   This API is only supported for H.264/H.265 encoding channels.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   this interfaceused for Sets the strong edge dering level for the JPEG encoding channel.
-   This interface must be called after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.
-   this interfaceonlyfor/to JPEGType's encoding channelvalid/effective  andonlyin/at dering\_modeis/for 1 case/conditionbefore  enough/sufficientcall。
-   强边去RING效应强度等级有四种： 
    -   OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_DERING\_LEVEL\_0, etc. level: default dering\_level value，when open or  closeglobal  removederingfunction whenMust set。
    -   OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_DERING\_LEVEL\_1, etc. level: relatively low 's  removeRing capability strength/power，relatively height 's sharp/clear degree。
    -   OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_DERING\_LEVEL\_2, etc. level: in/within , etc.'s  removeRing capability strength/power，in/within , etc.'s sharp/clear degree。
    -   OT\_VENC\_JPEG\_DERING\_LEVEL\_3, etc. level: relatively  strong/enhance's  removeRing capability strength/power，relatively low 's sharp/clear degree。

-   创建JPEG通道之后，JPEG通道默认处于dering\_mode=1的情况。当用户需要设置JPEG编码通道的强边去Ring效应强度等级，可以调用本接口，设置JPEG通道为不同的强度等级，从而得到需要的效果。
-   onlyHi3403V100/SS626V100supports。

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This API enables deblocking for the JPEG encoding channel.
-   This API improves block artifacts at lower qfactor (<50).
-   This interface must be called after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

**Requirements**

-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface sets JPEGand MJPEGencoding channelROIheight  levelattribute。
-   ROIparameter：
    -   idx: supportseach channel canSets 16 ROI，encoderaccording to 0～15's index numberROIperform management，idxindicates 's user Sets ROI's index number.ROIbetween can overlay each other, andwhen occurs overlay when, ROIbetween 's priority according to index number0～15provided in orderheight 。
    -   enable: specified current's ROIwhether enable。
    -   fg\_level：ROI区域的降码率等级，0\~15依次提高，等级越高降码率相对越多，图像质量损失也相对越多。
    -   bg\_level：非ROI区域的降码率等级，0\~15依次提高，等级越高降码率相对越多，图像质量损失也相对越多。
    -   rect: specified current's ROI's startingCoordinatesand  region'ssize 。ROI's starting pointCoordinatesmustin/at imagerange  within, and must16alignment；ROI region'slong width mustis 16alignmentand greater than 0；ROIregion mustin/at imagerange within 。
    -   onlysupports4 typenot  same's level, i.e., most multiple/more3 typenot  same's fg\_leveland 1 typenot  same's bg\_level。

-   this interfaceis an advanced interface，default not enableROI，user mustcallthis interfaceenableROI。
-   This interface must be set after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after the channel is created and before encoding starts, to reduce the number of calls during encoding.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.
